<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate the angle between the hands of a clock at $9.30$.
(Remember that when the minute hand is at half past, the hour hand has moved halfway between the $9$ and the $10$).

![papers/chigwell-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png](/assets/papers/chigwell-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png "papers/chigwell-school/2020/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate the angle between the hands of a clock at $9.30$.
(Remember that when the minute hand is at half past, the hour hand has moved halfway between the $9$ and the $10$).
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: The difference between every $5$ minutes of a clock is $30 \degree$.

- ABBR: The difference between minutes of a clock is $6 \degree$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$

                                        &= 105 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

**Just a minute**

Work out how many degrees the minute hand moves through between $07.30$ and $09.15$.

You can use the clock face to help you.

![papers/christs-hospital-school/2014/sample-paper-1/s02-q02-i00.png](/assets/papers/christs-hospital-school/2014/sample-paper-1/s02-q02-i00.png "papers/christs-hospital-school/2014/sample-paper-1/s02-q02-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">**Just a minute**
<emptyline>
Work out how many degrees the minute hand moves through between $07.30$ and $09.15$.
<emptyline>
You can use the clock face to help you.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: The difference between every $5$ minutes of a clock is $30 \degree$.

- ABBR: The difference between minutes of a clock is $6 \degree$.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}                   &= 9{:}30 \ \text{am} -  8 \ \text{am} \\\\
                                        &= 1 \times 60 \text{ minutes} + 45 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                        &= 60 + 45 \\\\
                                        &= 105 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle}                           &= 105 \times 6 \\\\
                                        &= 630 \degree \\\\
                                        &= 270 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$270 \degree$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>

</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                                        &= 105 \text{ minutes} \\\\

<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every hour.

- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $0.5 \degree$ every minute.

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}   &= 12{:}00 \ \text{pm} -  5{:}00 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                        &= 5 \times 60  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= 300 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle moved}     &= 300 \times 0.5 \\\\
                        &= 150 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$150 \degree$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Through how many degrees does the minute hand of a clock turn between $0900$ and $0945?$
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                        &= 270 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

$270 \degree$

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every hour.

- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $0.5 \degree$ every minute.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}   &= 3{:}50 \ \text{pm} -  2{:}30 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                        &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 20 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= 80 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle moved}     &= 80 \times 0.5 \\\\
                        &= 40 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$40 \degree$

**Question**

In $25 \ \text{minutes}$, through what angle does the minute hand of a clock pass?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

- ABBR: The minute hand of a clock moves by $6 \degree$ every minute.
</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}   &= 25 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle moved}     &= 25 \times 6 \\\\
                        &= 150 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

$150 \degree$

**Question**

In the diagram $AB$ is a straight line.
Calculate the value of angle $a \degree$.

![papers/kings-college-school/2017/specimen-paper-1/s01-q02-s02-i00.png](/assets/papers/kings-college-school/2017/specimen-paper-1/s01-q02-s02-i00.png "papers/kings-college-school/2017/specimen-paper-1/s01-q02-s02-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the diagram $AB$ is a straight line.
Calculate the value of angle $a \degree$.
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
48 + 3a  &=  180 \\\\
3a       &=  180 - 48 \\\\
3a       &=  132 \\\\
a        &=  \dfrac{132}{3} \\\\
a        &=  \dfrac{44 \times 3}{3} \\\\
a        &=  \dfrac{44 \times \cancel{3}}{\cancel{3}} \\\\
a        &=  44 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$44 \degree$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

Calculate the length of one side.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the length of one side $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
4a  &=  96 \\\\
a   &=  \dfrac{96}{4} \\\\
a   &=  \dfrac{24 \times 4}{4} \\\\
a   &=  \dfrac{24 \times \cancel{4}}{\cancel{4}} \\\\
a   &=  24 \text{ cm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many degrees has the minute hand moved between $3{:}55 \ \text{pm}$ and $5{:}15 \ \text{pm}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many degrees has the minute hand moved between $3{:}55 \ \text{pm}$ and $5{:}15 \ \text{pm}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: The minute hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every minute.

- ABBR: The minute hand of a clock moves by $6 \degree$ every minute.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}   &= 5{:}15 \text{ pm} - 3{:}55 \text{ pm} \\\\ 
                        &= 1 \times 60 \text{ minutes} +  20 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= 60 + 20 \\\\
                        &= 80 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle moved}     &= 80 \times 6 \\\\
                        &= 240 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$240 \degree$

</div>
</div>

(Remember: the hour hand will have moved on since $8{:}00!$)

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $417 \ \text{minutes}$ in hours and minutes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $417 \ \text{minutes}$ in hours and minutes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 417 \text{ minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac{417}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
&= \dfrac{417}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
&= 6\dfrac{57}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
&= 6 \text{ hours } 57 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$6 \text{ hours } 57 \text{ minutes}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

What is $327 \ \text{minutes}$ in hours and minutes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $327 \ \text{minutes}$ in hours and minutes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 327 \text{ minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac{327}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
&= \dfrac{327}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
&= 5\dfrac{27}{60} \text{ hours} \\\\
&= 5 \text{ hours } 27 \text{ minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A twenty-four hour digital watch shows only hours and minutes. How many times each day does the watch show at least two fours simultaneously?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A twenty-four hour digital watch shows only hours and minutes. How many times each day does the watch show at least two fours simultaneously?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

When the hour is $04$ or $14$, the minutes can be $04, 14, 24, 34, 40-49$ and $54$ which is $15$ possiblities for each hour

For the remaining $22$ hours, the minutes must be $44$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total}   &=  2 \times 15 + 22 \\\\
               &=  30 + 22 \\\\
               &=  52
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$52$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">When the hour is $04$ or $14$, the minutes can be $04, 14, 24, 34, 40-49$ and $54$ which is $15$ possiblities for each hour
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

\begin{aligned}
\text{Total}   &=  2 \times 15 + 22 \\\\
               &=  30 + 22 \\\\
               &=  52
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Option Raw</strong>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the angle between the hands of a clock at $2{:}30 \ \text{pm}$. (Remember that the hour hand moves as well) 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the angle between the hands of a clock at $2{:}30 \ \text{pm}$. (Remember that the hour hand moves as well) 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

- ABBR: The difference between minutes of a clock is $6 \degree$.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Minutes between hands of clock}  &= 20 - 2.5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                        &= 17.5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle}                           &= 17.5 \times 6 \\\\
                                        &= 105 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$105 \degree$

\text {Minutes between hands of clock}  &= 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle}                           &= 5 \times 6 \\\\
                                        &= 30 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

**Question**

$9 \ \text{a.m.}$ and $10 \ \text{a.m.}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$9 \ \text{a.m.}$ and $10 \ \text{a.m.}$
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every hour.

- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $0.5 \degree$ every minute.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}   &= 10{:}00 \ \text{am} -  9{:}00 \ \text{am} \\\\
                        &= 1 \times 60  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= 60 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle moved}     &= 60 \times 0.5 \\\\
                        &= 30 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

**Question**

A clock shows $2\ \text{o'clock}$, with the minute hand on $12$ and the hour hand on $2$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many times does the minute hand go past the hour hand between $2.00 \ \text{pm}$ and $7.00 \ \text{pm}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many times does the minute hand go past the hour hand between $2.00 \ \text{pm}$ and $7.00 \ \text{pm}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The hands overlap about every $65$ minutes

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total minutes}              &=  7{:}00 \text{ pm} - 2{:}00 \text{ pm} \\\\
                                  &=  5 \times 60 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                                  &=  300 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text{Number of times overlaped}  &= \dfrac{300}{65} \\\\
                                  &\approx 4
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What time will the clock show when the hour hand has turned through an angle of $60 \degree ?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What time will the clock show when the hour hand has turned through an angle of $60 \degree ?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: The minute hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every minute.

- ABBR: The minute hand of a clock moves by $6 \degree$ every minute.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of minutes passed}  &= \dfrac{60}{6} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{10 \times 6}{6} \\\\
                                 &= \dfrac{10 \times \cancel{6}}{\cancel{6}} \\\\
                                 &= 10 \text { minutes} \\\\
\text{Time}                      &= 2{:}00 \text{ pm} + 10  \text { minutes} \\\\
                                 &= 2{:}10 \text{ pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2{:}10 \text{ pm}$

</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Through what angle will the minute hand have turned from $2\ \text{o'clock}$, when the clock shows $10\ \text{o'clock}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Through what angle will the minute hand have turned from $2\ \text{o'clock}$, when the clock shows $10\ \text{o'clock}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

- ABBR: The minute hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every minute.

- ABBR: The minute hand of a clock moves by $6 \degree$ every minute.

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}   &= 10{:}00 \text{ pm} - 2{:}00 \text{ pm} \\\\
                        &= 8 \times 60 \text{ minutes} \\\\
                        &= 240 \text{ minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle moved}     &= 240 \times 6 \\\\
                        &= 1440 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

\end{aligned}

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Through what angle will the hour hand have turned from $2\ \text{o'clock}$, when the clock shows $6{:}30?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Through what angle will the hour hand have turned from $2\ \text{o'clock}$, when the clock shows $6{:}30?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every hour.

- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $0.5 \degree$ every minute.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}   &= 6{:}30 \ \text{pm} -  2{:}00 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                        &= 4 \times 60  \ \text{minutes} + 30  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= 240 + 30 \\\\
                        &= 270 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle moved}     &= 270 \times 0.5 \\\\
                        &= 135 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$135 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many degrees does the **hour** hand move between $1{:}10\ \text{am}$ and $4{:}30\ \text{am}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many degrees does the **hour** hand move between $1{:}10\ \text{am}$ and $4{:}30\ \text{am}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $30 \degree$ every hour.

- ABBR: The hour hand of a clock moves by $0.5 \degree$ every minute.
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total minutes}   &= 4{:}30 \ \text{am} -  1{:}10 \ \text{am} \\\\
                        &= 2 \times 60  \ \text{minutes} + 80  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= 120 + 80 \\\\
                        &= 200 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Angle moved}     &= 200 \times 0.5 \\\\
                        &= 100 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$100 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

</code></pre>
</div>

                        &= 2 \times 60  \ \text{minutes} + 80  \ \text{minutes} \\\\

                        &= 200 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
